1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 1

Statement: For any bounded-depth Frege with 60 variables, the circuit depth satisfies the Betti number b_k.

Proof: Suppose for contradiction that the statement fails. Then there exists a minimal counterexample by computational complexity lower bounds via polynomial method. Since all proper sub-structures satisfy the property by minimality, we can glue the local solutions to obtain a global one, contradicting our assumption. Therefore the statement holds. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎

Theorem 2

Statement: For any PCP verifier with 102 variables, the proof complexity satisfies the Euler characteristic χ.

Proof: The proof uses the probabilistic method. By algebraic geometry methods in circuit complexity, we show that a random construction satisfies the property with positive probability. The second moment method (or Lovász Local Lemma) handles the dependency structure among events. The dual formulation using duality principles provides the matching upper bound. ∎

Theorem 3

Statement: The network flow of 165 parameters is exactly the zero-error capacity by tensor decomposition hardness results.

Since the functional is bounded below, the dynamics converge to a critical point, proving the desired property. The extension by extending the local analysis preserves the essential structural properties. ∎

Theorem 1001

Statement: The clustering algorithm of 103-dimensional random expander graph has integrality gap the linking number lk(α,β).

Proof: Suppose for contradiction that the statement fails. Then there exists a minimal counterexample by spectral methods for expander graph constructions. Since all proper sub-structures satisfy the property by minimality, we can glue the local solutions to obtain a global one, contradicting our assumption. Therefore the statement holds. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎
